- **github-orchestrator**: GitHub integration specialist. Posts agent review results as PR comments, creates issues from audit reports, manages labels and milestones. - **github-orchestrator:actions-optimizer**: Use this agent to measure and cut CI time and spend — it checks cache hit rates, finds unnecessary job serialization, prunes matrices, adds concurrency groups and path filters, and hardens workflow permissions. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/actions-optimizer.md))
- **github-orchestrator:adversarial-verifier**: Use this agent to adversarially verify a single review finding. Its job is to refute the claim, not confirm it — it reads the surrounding code for guards and constraints that make the finding wrong, and defaults to refuted when uncertain. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/adversarial-verifier.md))
- **github-orchestrator:agent-delegator**: Use this agent to judge whether work should be delegated to the Copilot cloud agent or an agentic workflow, to make the issue specific enough to succeed, and to track delegated pull requests back through the normal gates. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/agent-delegator.md))
- **github-orchestrator:api-contract-reviewer**: Use this agent as the API contract lens of the review board. It finds breaking changes to HTTP APIs, exported types, database schemas, event payloads, and config, and requires a stated migration path. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/api-contract-reviewer.md))
- **github-orchestrator:branch-strategist**: Use this agent to decide where a branch should be cut from, what it should be named per repository convention, and when a branch needs to be refreshed against its base. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/branch-strategist.md))
- **github-orchestrator:build-doctor**: Use this agent to fix build, compile, typecheck, and lint failures. It finds the root cause and repairs it rather than suppressing the diagnostic, and it reproduces locally before claiming a fix. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/build-doctor.md))
- **github-orchestrator:changelog-scribe**: Use this agent to generate Keep a Changelog entries and human release notes from a commit range, written from the reader's perspective with migration instructions for every breaking change. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/changelog-scribe.md))
- **github-orchestrator:ci-triage-analyst**: Use this agent to read failing CI logs and classify each failure by cause before any fix is attempted, so that infrastructure flakes are not "fixed" by editing source code. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/ci-triage-analyst.md))
- **github-orchestrator:conflict-resolver**: Use this agent to predict which open pull requests will conflict before they do, and to resolve existing conflicts by reconstructing a result that satisfies both sides rather than picking ours or theirs. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/conflict-resolver.md))
- **github-orchestrator:correctness-reviewer**: Use this agent as the correctness lens of the review board. It hunts logic errors, null paths, off-by-ones, race conditions, and swallowed errors, and reports nothing it cannot demonstrate with a concrete failing input. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/correctness-reviewer.md))
- **github-orchestrator:dedup-detective**: Use this agent to detect duplicate issues semantically — matching on symptom, stack trace, and affected surface rather than title wording — and to search closed issues, where a previously-closed duplicate is itself a signal. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/dedup-detective.md))
- **github-orchestrator:dependency-steward**: Use this agent to report dependency health and drive upgrades — batching by risk so each pull request has a single failure hypothesis, keeping the lockfile in sync, and flagging unmaintained or suspicious packages. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/dependency-steward.md))
- **github-orchestrator:docs-reviewer**: Use this agent as the documentation lens of the review board. It finds docs the diff silently invalidated, undocumented breaking changes, and missing changelog entries — it does not ask for comments on obvious code. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/docs-reviewer.md))
- **github-orchestrator:dora-analyst**: Use this agent to compute deployment frequency, lead time, change failure rate, and time to restore from real repository history, decomposed so that every regression points to a specific stage and cause. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/dora-analyst.md))
- **github-orchestrator:epic-decomposer**: Use this agent to decompose a large issue or goal into sub-issues that each deliver standalone value, are sized under the review budget, and are linked and ordered by dependency. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/epic-decomposer.md))
- **github-orchestrator:flake-detective**: Use this agent to confirm whether a test is genuinely flaky by correlating results across runs, identify the specific source of nondeterminism, and quarantine it behind a tracked issue rather than silently skipping it. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/flake-detective.md))
- **github-orchestrator:gh-advisor**: Use this agent to get prioritized, evidence-backed recommendations on what to do next across pull requests, CI, issues, security alerts, and releases — which workflow to launch and which agents to deploy. Read-only; it advises, it never mutates GitHub. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/gh-advisor.md))
- **github-orchestrator:gh-orchestrator**: Use this agent to run a full GitHub delivery loop end to end — it decomposes the goal, dispatches the delivery, review, CI, and release teams, holds the blackboard, enforces merge policy, and does not report success until the change is merged and green. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/gh-orchestrator.md))
- **github-orchestrator:hotspot-scout**: Use this agent to identify change-failure hotspots — files that change often and are often followed by a fix or revert — and diagnose the structural reason each one is fragile. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/hotspot-scout.md))
- **github-orchestrator:issue-triager**: Use this agent to triage issues — classifying type and severity from evidence in the report, identifying what is missing for reproduction, estimating size, and routing to an owner. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/issue-triager.md))
- **github-orchestrator:merge-marshal**: Use this agent as the last step before any merge. It re-checks required checks, review quorum, unresolved threads, mergeability, and protected paths against live GitHub state, and refuses to merge when a gate fails. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/merge-marshal.md))
- **github-orchestrator:ownership-mapper**: Use this agent to find ownership drift, uncovered and orphaned CODEOWNERS paths, and bus-factor-one modules, and to route reviewers on a pull request by ownership, recent authorship, and current load. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/ownership-mapper.md))
- **github-orchestrator:performance-reviewer**: Use this agent as the performance lens of the review board. It targets complexity regressions that get worse with scale — N+1 queries, unbounded work, missing indexes, and allocation in hot paths — not micro-optimizations. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/performance-reviewer.md))
- **github-orchestrator:pr-author**: Use this agent to author or refresh a pull request title and body. It reads the real diff rather than the stated intent, fills the repository's PR template if one exists, and never includes credentials, tokens, or internal hostnames. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/pr-author.md))
- **github-orchestrator:project-steward**: Use this agent to inspect or update a GitHub Projects board — resolving field and single-select option IDs, adding and updating items, auditing board hygiene, and identifying automation that a built-in project workflow already covers. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/project-steward.md))
- **github-orchestrator:release-conductor**: Use this agent to run a release — computing the version bump from commit history, verifying every readiness gate against live state, and tagging and publishing only when all of them pass. ([TheLobbi/claude](https://github.com/TheLobbi/claude/blob/HEAD/plugins/delivery-orchestrator/agents/release-conductor.md))
- **github-orchestrator:repo-cartographer**: Use this agent to produce a structural map of a repository — entry points, module boundaries, dependency direction, build and test topology, and where the risky code lives — so other agents can act without re-exploring. - **github-scheduling**: Every pull request and push to `main` or `maint/*` runs the full matrix in `.github/workflows/ci-speed-proof.yml`; prose-only changes run seven common checks. No workflow runs on a schedule.
